                                            REPUBLIC OF ZAMBIA

                       MINISTRY OF MINES AND MINERALS DEVELOPMENT

                                                PUBLIC NOTICE

   DEFAULT NOTICE FOR NON-COMPLIANCE TO CONDITIONS OF GRANT OF MINING
                                        RIGHTS AND NON-MINING RIGHTS

The general public is hereby notified that the Mining Licensing Committee (MLC)
defaulted a total of 3,429 mining and non-mining rights categorized as Artisanal Mining
Rights (1,083), Small Scale Mining Licenses (442), Large Scale Mining Licenses (145),
Small Scale Exploration License (783), Large Scale Exploration License (813), and
Mineral Processing License (163). The defaulting is in accordance with Section 72 of the
Mines and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015, following failure to adhere to the
conditions of grant of mining and non-mining rights, contrary to the provisions of the
Mines and Minerals Development Act No.11 of 2015, read together with the Mines and
Minerals Development (General) Regulations of 2016. In particular, the breaches are as
follows:

    1) Failure to submit comprehensive quarterly reports to Geological Survey and Mine
         Safety Departments contrary to Regulation 49(1)(a) of the Mines and Minerals
         Development (General) Regulations of 2016 and attracting a penalty fine of three
         hundred thousand penalty units translating to K120,000.00 in monetary form, in
         accordance with Section 108(2) of the Mines and Minerals Development Act No.
         11 of 2015

    2) Failure to register a pegging certificate within one hundred and eighty (180) days
         of grant of licence contrary to Sections 18(2) of the Mines and Minerals
         Development Act No. 11 of 2015 attracting a penalty fine of three hundred
         thousand penalty units translating to K120,000.00 in accordance with Section
         108(2) of the Mines and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015 respectively

    3) Failure to make payment of annual area charges on or before the anniversary of
         grant of mining right, contrary to Section 77 of the Mines and Minerals
         Development Act No. 11 of 2015 and Regulation 57(3) of the Mines and Minerals
         Development (General) Regulation of 2016 which attract a penalty fine of five
         hundred thousand penalty units translating to K200,000.00 in monetary form, in
         accordance with Regulation 57(4) of the Mines and Minerals Development
         (General) Regulation of 2016.
    4) Failure to submit an approved decision letter for exploration operation in respect
         of the environmental project brief (EPB) by the Zambia Environmental
         Management Agency (ZEMA), contrary to Section 25(1)(a) of the Mines and
         Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015

    5) Failure to commence exploration operations in accordance with the approved
         program of operations within six (6) months of grant of the large-scale
         exploration licence contrary to Regulation 55(2) of the Mines and Minerals
         Development (General) Regulations of 2016

    6) Failure to submit annual reports to Mines Development and Mines Safety
         Departments not later than 28th February of each year on the operations
         conducted during the previous year ending on 31st December, contrary to
         Regulation 51 of the Mines and Minerals Development (General) Regulations of
         2016 which attract a penalty fine amounting to three hundred thousand penalty
         units translating to K120,000.00, in accordance with Section 108(2) of the Mines
         and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015

    7) Failure to submit monthly mineral production returns not later than fifteenth
         day of each month in respect to the preceding month, contrary to Regulation 50
         of the Mines and Minerals Development (General) Regulations of 2016 which
         attract a penalty fine amounting to three hundred thousand penalty units
         translating to K120,000.00 in monetary form, in accordance with Section 108(2)
         of the Mines and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015

    8) Failure to commence mining operations in accordance with the approved
         program of operations of the Large-Scale Mining Right, contrary to Section
         35(1)(b) of the Mines and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015 and
         Regulation 55(2) of the Mines and Minerals Development (General) Regulations
         of 2016

    9) Failure to submit future program of mining operation not later than the last day
         of November of each year for the ensuing year, contrary to Regulation 52(1) of
         Mines and Minerals Development (General) Regulations of 2016

    10) Failure to submit audited financial report(s) within three months of the end of
         each financial year, contrary to Regulation 54 of Mines and Minerals Development
         (General) Regulations of 2016

    11) Failure to make payment into the Environmental Protection Fund (EPF) contrary
         to Section 86(3) of the Mines and Minerals Development Act No. 11 of 2015.

In view of the foregoing and in accordance with Section 72(2) of the Mines and Minerals
Development Act No. 11 of 2015, all the underlisted non-compliant mining and non-
mining rights are directed to show reasons why their licenses should not be revoked by
the Mining Licensing Committee (MLC) within a period of thirty (30) days.
Default notices outlining the specific breaches will be sent to the respective company
registered email addresses or hard copies can be collected at Mining Cadastre
Department and Regional Mining Bureaux not later than 27th June, 2025.

Take this as an official notification.

                                              Samuel C. Maango
                                                     Director

                                      Mining Cadastre Department
